Theorem 211.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The information operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 136. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 205 with 3 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to information theory, we decompose the problem into 136 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 205. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(3/n). QED.
